Singapore).
This report and [the final handoff receipt](../experiments/overnight-20261014/coordinator/final-handoff.json)
separate terminal evidence from calculations still in progress. Source, patches,
failed attempts, exact verification domains and compact logs are preserved under
`experiments/overnight-20261014/`. Large streams remain on the server and in ignored research output.

**The ten-hour window is complete. No original submission-ready improvement
has qualified in any track.** The approved bank-only pipeline remains active
with a documented handoff; its whole-point result is pending.

## Best verified local results

| Objective | Best verified local result | Provenance |
|---|---|---|
| Minimum peak qubits | Q1234, T761,658 | Historical complete c5 public reproduction; not rerun this cycle |
| Minimum rounded mean executed Toffolis | T761,156, Q1235 | New unchanged b3 public reproduction; all9,024 shots pass |
| Minimum product | 939,885,972, Q1234/T761,658 | Historical complete c5 public reproduction |

The b3 reproduction has zero value, phase and ancilla failure channels, stream
`0f10de57f45349c89163faf6e5ab0694aec3d3bfa720f10ee8b185a9fb2914ce`.
The historical c5 stream is
`49b35cf7640ebd529d76894eb8bef93ebf57e7e970fe14b3c27c8c5feb6e73e4`.
Both have independent counterexamples. A complete passing finite benchmark
packet remains distinct from correctness on every admitted input.

The final public snapshot has1,564 submissions,1,223 eligible records and45
Pareto pairs. Public leaders are Q693, T761,156 and product939,396,074
(Q1234/T761,261, revision441d9ef9). Strict targets are Q692, T761,155 and
product939,396,073; atQ1234 the product target is T761,260, atQ1235 T760,644.
[Official dated comparison and hashes](data/latest-leaderboard.json).
The public leader's note describes census deletion, narrowed approximate compares
and nonce selection. These assumptions are comparison context and have not been
adopted. Failed, pending and component-only experiments are absent from README plots.

## What the research established

**Qubits.** Paired replay localized the original compact failures to inherited
arithmetic defects on the same inputs and measurement transcripts. Invalid shared
build-cache diagnostics were retained and excluded. Quotient-owner and carry
audits led to independent frozen prototypes. N275-v2 completed the unchanged
9,024-shot benchmark with one value failure, one phase-garbage batch and one
ancilla-garbage batch; it has no qualified score. N274-v4 generated a diagnostic
stream, but its known shot2379 arithmetic failure blocks grading/qualification.

A separate bank-swap-v2 operator preserves the same admitted arithmetic using
reversible transfer between quotient and reserve storage. Independent source
review checks stable controls, clean scratch, the Euclidean invariant at both
reciprocal calls and inverse PH normalization. It passes17 native tests and17
exact phase fixtures,704 fixed-input all-measurement audits. These are component
proofs; whole-point qualification remains pending.
[Independent source argument](../experiments/overnight-20261014/toffoli/bankswap-independent-review-v2.md).

The repaired HT25-v9 restores public mixed-HR23 capacity and corrects quotient
parking, source masking, carry ownership and the final inverse's phase-control
lifetime. It passes16 selected native tests and16 exact decision-diagram tests
covering576 fixed-input all-measurement instances, including quotient boundaries,
the physical shot2379 column, delayed-HR counterexamples and done-column states.
These proofs quantify future measurement outcomes on their fixed component
inputs; they are not a full-point or all-curve proof. Quotients at2^25 remain a
preserved expected-negative boundary, and ideal packet arithmetic still identifies
precision limitations. Full generation is separate from qualification.
[Source, domain and owner argument](../experiments/overnight-20261014/qubits/HT25_DOMAIN_AND_OWNERS.md).
That frozen component report predates generation; later stream/job status is
recorded in the final generation receipt and handoff, rather than rewriting it.

**Toffolis.** Complete constructor analysis strengthened the necessary bound to
at least27 ANDs for the specified all14-input XOR/AND value contract. The result
depends on trusted Z3 UNSAT with independently audited encoding, not an exported
formal proof certificate. Changed consumer, arithmetic producer and carry-fusion
searches closed selected families without a complete saving. The late-fold lazy
cleanup component retains an expected7/8-Toffoli saving on its admitted local
domain, with full phase/dirty-classical checks, but no whole-point score.
Final cleanup analysis replayed105 production plans:99 feasible plans showed
zero mean saving and six new38-wire plans were infeasible. These results do not
prove a global optimum. [Final Toffoli report](../experiments/overnight-20261014/toffoli/final-track-report.md).

**Product and correctness.** Original raw-body probes located deterministic
rail109 signed overflow and fold75/104 missing-borrow faults before postpasses.
Source-bound exact all-future-measurement proofs preserved the same faults.
The fold57 repair corrects the affected104 body on16 literal original-prefix
states, including live classical passengers, incoming phase, inverse and scratch
checks. Past measurements are fixed; future outcomes are quantified only within
that body. The full repaired builder fails its existing allocator fingerprint at tick111:
the free-wire set is preserved but order differs. No adapter, complete stream,
full benchmark or score was claimed.

Exact Boolean/ANF simplification, unconditional-reset recoloring and constant
measurement scans found no complete width/gate improvement. Their scoped
negative results, ABI initialization mistake and later corrections remain
preserved so future work can avoid repeating them.

## Completed evaluations, active calculations and exact collection

Use SSH alias `smu_server`, hostLHC-104-070, workspace
`/home/hclau/ecdsa_research/overnight-20261014`. Jobs use `nohup`, dedicated
logs, recorded PIDs/exits and frozen source/stream bindings. Four build threads
and one library thread are the default. Unrelated jobs and chemistry are preserved.
Refresh CPU, memory, processes and schedulers before launching anything further.

```bash
ssh smu_server
cd /home/hclau/ecdsa_research/overnight-20261014
python3 /home/hclau/ecdsa_research/server-preflight.py
ps -p 2805813,2812595 -o pid,etime,rss,%cpu,args
tail -30 qubits/evidence/headflag-v1-official-eval.log
cat qubits/evidence/headflag-v1-official-eval-native.exit
cat coordinator-headflag-collection-watch-v1/run.exit
cat coordinator-headflag-v1-terminal/receipt.json
tail -30 qubits/evidence/ht25-v9-full-build.log
cat qubits/evidence/ht25-v9-full-build.exit
cat qubits/evidence/ht25-v9-stream-metadata.json
cat qubits/evidence/ht25-v9-parser-admission.json
```

Missing exit files mean pending. Headflag evaluator2662310 completed at19:46UTC with exit1 after all9,024
shots: four value failures, one phase-garbage batch and four ancilla-garbage
batches; first mismatch644. Its elapsed time was2:20:20 and maximumRSS
191,446,700KiB. Detached collector2740181 completed exit0 at19:46:49UTC,
checking exact source, protected binary and stream hashes without rerunning the
benchmark. The completed receipt has no qualified score.
[Full failure receipt](../experiments/overnight-20261014/coordinator/headflag-v1-full-failed-receipt.json).
These two PIDs are terminal; their files are retained for read-only collection.
Available memory rose to294,100,316,160bytes at19:46:52UTC, but this snapshot
is not a reservation. The unrelated server workloads remain preserved.

HT25-v9 generation wrapper2785256 completed exit0 at19:57:32UTC under16GiB,
without allocating the giant operation vector or running the scorer. Bounded
metadata reports2,499,445,456 operations and692 referenced qubits; the parser
admits the count, and the diagnostic stream hash is
`3b64970ffab83deb99c0f74d6277879f3ec33d157a765b5f47ba7bd6f3ea7e1d`.
The requested operation vector would be139,968,945,536bytes. These generated
counts are not scored results. Its evaluator is prepared but not launched;
whole-point validation is planned only for the separately checked bank-swap
variant, avoiding duplicate full runs. Its patch is
`2d1c9652a8083d1fb1d7c2b7214c6051fb79fde003073426dda9367998b316c0`.
Verify source/protected-source bindings, serializer462-operation equality,
stream SHA256, maximum referenced width and parser admission against4billion
operations. A parser-admitted generation is not a passing evaluation. Any
future full benchmark needs the protected unchanged evaluator, unique build
directory, sufficient refreshed available memory and the sole-large-job lock.
Do not rerun a completed benchmark or choose its packet/nonce.

Bank-swap-v2 full-generation wrapper2802421 completed exit0 at20:13:14UTC
under16GiB/four build threads/one library thread. Its stream has2,472,354,640
operations,692 referenced qubits and SHA256
`03cb7a3f0cc5bbb1c4d07a3cfed809e3623137425ebd7c725f158737f4a77739`.
The count is parser-admitted and its requested vector is138,451,859,840bytes.
These are generated diagnostics, with no scored result.

## Next experiments, in priority order

1. Collect the guarded bank-swap generation/benchmark terminal result without
   launching a duplicate. Native17/exact-phase17 component verification is
   complete; whole-point qualification remains pending. Replay the preserved
   failed headflag packet on the same inputs and measurements for any further
   diagnosis. Keep the completed V9 diagnostic stream and all source versions.
2. Address quotient/search precision and domains exposed by shot2379 and the
   ideal-model boundary failures. Fixed-input success cannot justify removing
   capacity or claiming every-input correctness.
3. Develop a semantically justified allocator-order adapter for fold57, preserving
   every protected assertion and binding. Complete whole-point validation before
   resuming resource tuning; the current repair itself increases local gate cost.
4. Seek a changed constructor/consumer contract with a complete reversible
   saving beyond the scoped27-AND bound. Include live carries, scratch and phase
   cleanup, and measure complete mean executed cost.
